# Content parity — old public site (16a9b58) → Lagoon redesign

Every piece of copy the old public site showed, where it lives now and its status.
Generated from the dictionaries the pages read (`locales/public/*.ts`, `lib/legal-i18n.ts`, `lib/i18n.ts` for PublicNav/PublicFooter),
so the four language columns are exactly what visitors see. “Old langs” says which languages the old site had for the item;
where the old site was English-only (status page, Wix app, error pages, accessibility statement, testimonials in their own language)
the other columns are new translations. Arabic is Modern Standard Arabic (old colloquial wording rewritten with the same meaning).

**Status:** `restored` = missing from the redesign at 394b7ad and brought back; `already present` = the redesign kept it.
Nothing is dropped. `(en)` in a French/Turkish column means that item falls back to English (never to Arabic).

New copy added by the redesign is kept as well (home product rows `mk_row*`, product preview, `mk_home_h1` lead line above the product rows,
`mk_pricing_title` eyebrow, `mk_cta_title`/`mk_cta_note` in the closing CTA, “Save up to n%”, plan-data comparison table, blog TOC/related posts, legal print/TOC chrome).

## Claims for the owner to review

These figures and promises are shown exactly as on the old site:
- Home stats: **10M+** messages per month, **99.9%** platform uptime, **1s** message delivery, **50+** countries served.
- Pricing trust badges: **10,000+** businesses, **500M+** messages sent, **99.9%** uptime SLA, **4.9★** average rating; “Trusted by thousands of businesses”.
- “Join thousands of businesses…” (home CTA, pricing CTA, about CTA).
- **14-day free trial** on paid plans, “No credit card required”, “Setup in under 5 minutes”, “14-day Pro trial” (blog CTA), 14-day money-back (refund policy).
- About: “Serving businesses in **50+ countries**”, **99.9% uptime guarantee** (values, hosting), founded **2024**, **4** products & services.
- Status page: “**99.9% uptime — last 90 days**”.
- Testimonials: 24 named reviews with figures (e.g. −34% cart abandonment, 8x open rates, −41%/−52% no-shows, €2,000/month reselling), “verified customer” badges, a “N businesses joined this week” counter (34–61, computed from the date) and rotating “just connected WhatsApp” notes.
- Features / FAQ: AI “speaks 50+ languages”, Zapier “5,000+ apps”, Meta Cloud API “zero ban risk” / “never at risk of being banned”, “Unlimited scale”.
- Hero cards: 98% read rate, delivered in 1s, replied in 0.3s (illustrative).

| Mission + Company | mission_body | We believe every business, from a small shop in Cairo to an enterprise in London, deserves powerful and affordable communication tools. WhatsApp has over 3 billion users worldwide. WABEK makes WhatsApp automation accessible to everyone, with no technical expertise required. We handle the complexity so you can focus on what matters: growing your business and serving your customers. | نؤمن بأن كل شركة، من محل صغير في القاهرة إلى مؤسسة في لندن، تستحق أدوات تواصل قوية وبأسعار معقولة. يضم واتساب أكثر من 3 مليار مستخدم حول العالم. يجعل WABEK أتمتة واتساب في متناول الجميع، دون الحاجة لخبرة تقنية. نحن نتولى التعقيد حتى تتمكن من التركيز على ما يهم: تنمية عملك وخدمة عملائك. | Nous croyons que chaque entreprise, d'une petite boutique au Caire à une grande entreprise à Londres, mérite des outils de communication puissants et abordables. WhatsApp compte plus de 3 milliards d'utilisateurs dans le monde. WABEK rend l'automatisation WhatsApp accessible à tous, sans expertise technique requise. | Kahire'deki küçük bir dükkandan Londra'daki büyük bir şirkete kadar her işletmenin güçlü ve uygun fiyatlı iletişim araçlarına hak kazandığına inanıyoruz. WhatsApp'ın dünya genelinde 3 milyardan fazla kullanıcısı var. WABEK, teknik uzmanlık gerektirmeden WhatsApp otomasyonunu herkes için erişilebilir kılıyor. | en ar fr tr | pages/about.tsx › Mission / Company cards — `about:mission_body` | already present |
| Mission + Company | company_title | About WEBEK LTD | نبذة عن WEBEK LTD | À propos de WEBEK LTD | WEBEK LTD Hakkında | en ar fr tr | pages/about.tsx › Mission / Company cards — `about:company_title` | already present |
| Mission + Company | company_body | WEBEK LTD is a United Kingdom registered technology company (Company number: 16326894), headquartered at 71-75 Shelton Street, Covent Garden, London. Founded to bridge the gap between enterprise-level digital tools and everyday businesses, WEBEK builds products that are powerful yet simple. From web hosting and domain names to website design, mobile apps, and AI-powered automation, WEBEK LTD serves clients across the UK, Middle East, Europe, and beyond. WABEK is our latest and most ambitious product, bringing world-class WhatsApp automation to businesses of all sizes. | WEBEK LTD هي شركة تقنية مسجلة في المملكة المتحدة (رقم الشركة: 16326894)، ومقرها في 71-75 شيلتون ستريت، كوفنت غاردن، لندن. تأسست لسد الفجوة بين الأدوات الرقمية على مستوى المؤسسات والشركات اليومية. تبني WEBEK منتجات قوية وبسيطة في آنٍ واحد. من استضافة الويب وأسماء النطاقات إلى تصميم المواقع وتطبيقات الجوال والأتمتة بالذكاء الاصطناعي، تخدم WEBEK LTD عملاء في المملكة المتحدة والشرق الأوسط وأوروبا وما هو أبعد. WABEK هو أحدث منتجاتنا وأكثرها طموحاً، يجلب أتمتة واتساب عالمية المستوى للشركات من جميع الأحجام. | WEBEK LTD est une société technologique enregistrée au Royaume-Uni (numéro: 16326894), dont le siège est au 71-75 Shelton Street, Covent Garden, Londres. Fondée pour combler le fossé entre les outils numériques d'entreprise et les petites entreprises, WEBEK construit des produits puissants et simples. De l'hébergement web aux noms de domaine, en passant par la conception de sites web, les applications mobiles et l'automatisation IA, WEBEK LTD sert des clients au Royaume-Uni, au Moyen-Orient, en Europe et au-delà. WABEK est notre produit le plus récent et le plus ambitieux. | WEBEK LTD, 71-75 Shelton Street, Covent Garden, Londra adresinde genel merkezi bulunan bir Birleşik Krallık teknoloji şirketidir (Şirket numarası: 16326894). Kurumsal düzeydeki dijital araçlarla günlük işletmeler arasındaki uçurumu kapatmak için kurulan WEBEK, güçlü ama basit ürünler üretir. Web barındırmadan alan adlarına, web sitesi tasarımından mobil uygulamalara ve yapay zeka destekli otomasyona kadar WEBEK LTD, İngiltere, Orta Doğu, Avrupa ve ötesindeki müşterilere hizmet veriyor. WABEK, her ölçekteki işletmeye dünya standartlarında WhatsApp otomasyonu sunan en yeni ve en iddialı ürünümüzdür. | en ar fr tr | pages/about.tsx › Mission / Company cards — `about:company_body` | already present |

| What is WABEK | wabek_body1 | WABEK is a complete WhatsApp Business automation platform built for companies that want to communicate smarter. Whether you run a small e-commerce store or manage a large enterprise support team, WABEK gives you the tools to automate conversations, send bulk campaigns, build chatbots, and reply to customers with AI, all through WhatsApp. | WABEK هي منصة أتمتة واتساب للأعمال الكاملة، مصممة للشركات التي تريد التواصل بذكاء أكبر. سواء كنت تدير متجراً إلكترونياً صغيراً أو تدير فريق دعم مؤسسي كبيراً، يمنحك WABEK الأدوات اللازمة لأتمتة المحادثات وإرسال حملات جماعية وبناء روبوتات دردشة والرد على العملاء، كل ذلك عبر واتساب. | WABEK est une plateforme complète d'automatisation WhatsApp Business conçue pour les entreprises qui veulent communiquer plus intelligemment. Que vous gériez une petite boutique en ligne ou une grande équipe de support, WABEK vous donne les outils pour automatiser les conversations, envoyer des campagnes, créer des chatbots et répondre aux clients. | WABEK, daha akıllı iletişim kurmak isteyen şirketler için tasarlanmış eksiksiz bir WhatsApp Business otomasyon platformudur. Küçük bir e-ticaret mağazası işletseniz de büyük bir kurumsal destek ekibini yönetseniz de WABEK, konuşmaları otomatikleştirme, toplu kampanyalar gönderme, chatbot oluşturma ve müşterilere WhatsApp üzerinden yanıt verme araçları sunar. | en ar fr tr | pages/about.tsx › What is WABEK — `about:wabek_body1` | already present |
| What is WABEK | wabek_body2 | WABEK supports both the official Meta WhatsApp Cloud API for businesses that need compliance and scale, and WhatsApp Web for those who want to get started quickly. With WABEK, you can manage multiple WhatsApp numbers, automate customer journeys, integrate with your website or CRM, and track every message with detailed analytics. | يدعم WABEK كلاً من واجهة برمجة تطبيقات Meta WhatsApp Cloud API الرسمية للشركات التي تحتاج إلى الامتثال والنطاق الواسع، وواتساب Web لمن يريد البدء بسرعة. مع WABEK، يمكنك إدارة أرقام واتساب متعددة وأتمتة رحلات العملاء والتكامل مع موقعك أو CRM وتتبع كل رسالة بتحليلات مفصلة. | WABEK prend en charge l'API Meta WhatsApp Cloud officielle pour les entreprises nécessitant conformité et évolutivité, ainsi que WhatsApp Web. Avec WABEK, vous pouvez gérer plusieurs numéros WhatsApp, automatiser les parcours clients et intégrer votre site web ou CRM. | WABEK, uyumluluk ve ölçek isteyen işletmeler için resmi Meta WhatsApp Cloud API'sini ve hızlı başlamak isteyenler için WhatsApp Web'i destekler. | Bullet lists | List l5 | Provide, improve, and maintain WABEK • Send OTP codes and security notifications • Process payments and manage subscriptions • Respond to support requests • Detect and prevent fraud and abuse • Comply with legal and regulatory obligations • Send product updates and necessary notifications | تقديم خدمة WABEK وتحسينها وصيانتها • إرسال رموز التحقق OTP وإشعارات الأمان • معالجة المدفوعات وإدارة الاشتراكات • الرد على طلبات الدعم ورسائل التواصل • اكتشاف الاحتيال وإساءة الاستخدام ومنعهما • الامتثال للالتزامات القانونية والتنظيمية • إرسال تحديثات المنتج والإشعارات الضرورية | Fournir, améliorer et maintenir WABEK • Envoyer des codes OTP et notifications de sécurité • Traiter les paiements et gérer les abonnements • Répondre aux demandes de support • Détecter et prévenir la fraude • Respecter les obligations légales • Envoyer les mises à jour produit nécessaires | WABEK'i sağlamak, geliştirmek ve sürdürmek • OTP kodları ve güvenlik bildirimleri göndermek • Ödemeleri işlemek ve abonelikleri yönetmek • Destek taleplerine yanıt vermek • Dolandırıcılığı tespit etmek ve önlemek • Yasal yükümlülüklere uymak • Ürün güncellemeleri ve bildirimler göndermek | en ar fr tr | pages/legal/privacy.tsx — `legal-i18n:privacy.lists.l5` | already present |
| Bullet lists | List l6 | Personal data deleted within 30 days • Contacts and campaign data deleted within 30 days • Message logs retained up to 90 days for fraud prevention • Billing data deleted after 7 years per legal requirements | البيانات الشخصية تُحذف خلال 30 يوماً • جهات الاتصال وبيانات الحملات خلال 30 يوماً • سجلات الرسائل تُحتفظ حتى 90 يوماً لمنع الاحتيال • بيانات الفواتير بعد 7 سنوات وفقاً للمتطلبات القانونية | Données personnelles supprimées dans les 30 jours • Contacts et campagnes supprimés dans les 30 jours • Journaux de messages conservés 90 jours max pour prévention • Données de facturation supprimées après 7 ans (exigence légale) | Kişisel veriler 30 gün içinde silinir • Kişiler ve kampanya verileri 30 gün içinde silinir • Mesaj kayıtları dolandırıcılık önleme için 90 güne kadar saklanır • Fatura verileri yasal gereğe uyarak 7 yıl sonra silinir | en ar fr tr | pages/legal/privacy.tsx — `legal-i18n:privacy.lists.l6` | already present |
| Bullet lists | List l7 | Right to access: export all your data from Account Settings • Right to rectification: edit your information from your profile • Right to erasure: permanently delete your account and all data • Right to portability: export your data in JSON/CSV • Right to object: unsubscribe from marketing communications | حق الوصول: تصدير جميع بياناتك من إعدادات الحساب • حق التصحيح: تعديل معلوماتك من ملفك الشخصي • حق الحذف: حذف حسابك وجميع بياناتك نهائياً • حق النقل: تصدير بياناتك بتنسيق JSON/CSV • حق الاعتراض: إلغاء الاشتراك في الاتصالات التسويقية | Droit d'accès : exportez vos données depuis les paramètres • Droit de rectification : modifiez vos informations dans votre profil • Droit à l'effacement : supprimez votre compte et toutes vos données • Droit à la portabilité : exportez en JSON/CSV • Droit d'opposition : désinscrivez-vous des communications marketing | Erişim hakkı: Hesap ayarlarından verilerinizi dışa aktarın • Düzeltme hakkı: Bilgilerinizi profilinizden düzenleyin • Silme hakkı: Hesabınızı ve tüm verilerinizi kalıcı olarak silin • Taşınabilirlik hakkı: JSON/CSV olarak dışa aktarın • İtiraz hakkı: Pazarlama iletişimlerinden çıkın | en ar fr tr | pages/legal/privacy.tsx — `legal-i18n:privacy.lists.l7` | already present |
| Bullet lists | List l8 | TLS 1.3 encryption for all data in transit • AES-256 encryption for data at rest • bcrypt password hashing (we never see your password) • Two-factor authentication (2FA) • Regular security audits and continuous monitoring • Daily encrypted backups | تشفير TLS 1.3 لجميع البيانات أثناء النقل • تشفير AES-256 للبيانات المحفوظة • تجزئة bcrypt لكلمات المرور (لا نراها أبداً) • مصادقة ثنائية العوامل (2FA) • مراجعات أمنية دورية ومراقبة مستمرة • نسخ احتياطية يومية مشفرة | Chiffrement TLS 1.3 en transit • Chiffrement AES-256 au repos • Hachage bcrypt des mots de passe • Authentification à deux facteurs (2FA) • Audits de sécurité réguliers • Sauvegardes quotidiennes chiffrées | Transit için TLS 1.3 şifrelemesi • Beklemedeki veriler için AES-256 • Şifre bcrypt karması • İki faktörlü kimlik doğrulama (2FA) • Düzenli güvenlik denetimleri • Günlük şifreli yedeklemeler | en ar fr tr | pages/legal/privacy.tsx — `legal-i18n:privacy.lists.l8` | already present |
| Bullet lists | List l9 | Authentication session (expires on logout) • Language preference and interface settings • CSRF token for security protection | جلسة المصادقة (تنتهي عند تسجيل الخروج) • تفضيل اللغة وإعدادات الواجهة • رمز CSRF لحماية الأمان | Session d'authentification (expire à la déconnexion) • Préférence de langue et paramètres • Jeton CSRF pour la sécurité | Kimlik doğrulama oturumu (çıkışta sona erer) • Dil tercihi ve arayüz ayarları • Güvenlik için CSRF jetonu | en ar fr tr | pages/legal/privacy.tsx — `legal-i18n:privacy.lists.l9` | already present |
